<file><path>tui-ts/src/components/StatusBar.tsx</path>
<content lines="1-126">
  1 | // Status bar - fixed at bottom, single line, always visible
  2 | 
  3 | import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
  4 | import { Box, Text } from 'ink';
  5 | import { useAppState } from '../store/appStore.js';
  6 | import { theme, glyphs } from '../theme.js';
  7 | 
  8 | export const StatusBar = React.memo(() => {
  9 |   const state = useAppState();
 10 |   const [cols, setCols] = useState(process.stdout.columns || 80);
 11 | 
 12 |   // Handle terminal resize
 13 |   useEffect(() => {
 14 |     const handleResize = () => {
 15 |       setCols(process.stdout.columns || 80);
 16 |     };
 17 | 
 18 |     process.stdout.on('resize', handleResize);
 19 |     return () => {
 20 |       process.stdout.off('resize', handleResize);
 21 |     };
 22 |   }, []);
 23 | 
 24 |   // Format numbers
 25 |   const formatTokens = (tokens: number): string => {
 26 |     if (tokens >= 1000) {
 27 |       return `${(tokens / 1000).toFixed(1)}k`;
 28 |     }
 29 |     return tokens.toString();
 30 |   };
 31 | 
 32 |   // Responsive layout based on terminal width
 33 |   const getStatusContent = () => {
 34 |     const isOffline = state.backendStatus === 'offline';
 35 |     const displayTokens = isOffline ? '--' : formatTokens(state.tokens);
 36 |     const displayQuota = isOffline ? '--' : `${state.quotaPercent}%`;
 37 |     const displayContext = isOffline ? '--' : `${state.ctxPercent}%`;
 38 | 
 39 |     if (cols < 80) {
 40 |       // Minimal: only model and tokens
 41 |       return (
 42 |         <>
 43 |           {isOffline && (
 44 |             <>
 45 |               <Text color={theme.error}>⚠ backend offline</Text>
 46 |               <Text color={theme.textDimmer}> · </Text>
 47 |             </>
 48 |           )}
 49 |           <Text color={theme.brand}>/model {state.model}</Text>
 50 |           <Text color={theme.textDimmer}> · </Text>
 51 |           <Text color={theme.text}>{displayTokens} tokens</Text>
 52 |         </>
 53 |       );
 54 |     }
 55 | 
 56 |     if (cols < 120) {
 57 |       // Medium: omit memory and session
 58 |       return (
 59 |         <>
 60 |           {isOffline && (
 61 |             <>
 62 |               <Text color={theme.error}>⚠ backend offline</Text>
 63 |               <Text color={theme.textDimmer}> · </Text>
 64 |             </>
 65 |           )}
 66 |           <Text color={theme.textDim}>workspace {state.workspace}</Text>
 67 |           <Text color={theme.textDimmer}> · </Text>
 68 |           <Text color={theme.accent}>branch {state.branch}</Text>
 69 |           <Text color={theme.textDimmer}> · </Text>
 70 |           <Text color={state.sandbox === 'no sandbox' ? theme.error : theme.accent}>
 71 |             sandbox {state.sandbox}
 72 |           </Text>
 73 |           <Text color={theme.textDimmer}> · </Text>
 74 |           <Text color={theme.brand}>/model {state.model}</Text>
 75 |           <Text color={theme.textDimmer}> · </Text>
 76 |           <Text color={theme.accent}>quota {displayQuota} used</Text>
 77 |           <Text color={theme.textDimmer}> · </Text>
 78 |           <Text color={theme.accent}>context {displayContext} used</Text>
 79 |           <Text color={theme.textDimmer}> · </Text>
 80 |           <Text color={theme.text}>{displayTokens} tokens</Text>
 81 |         </>
 82 |       );
 83 |     }
 84 | 
 85 |     // Full layout
 86 |     return (
 87 |       <>
 88 |         {isOffline && (
 89 |           <>
 90 |             <Text color={theme.error}>⚠ backend offline</Text>
 91 |             <Text color={theme.textDimmer}> · </Text>
 92 |           </>
 93 |         )}
 94 |         <Text color={theme.textDim}>workspace {state.workspace}</Text>
 95 |         <Text color={theme.textDimmer}> · </Text>
 96 |         <Text color={theme.accent}>branch {state.branch}</Text>
 97 |         <Text color={theme.textDimmer}> · </Text>
 98 |         <Text color={state.sandbox === 'no sandbox' ? theme.error : theme.accent}>
 99 |           sandbox {state.sandbox}
100 |         </Text>
101 |         <Text color={theme.textDimmer}> · </Text>
102 |         <Text color={theme.brand}>/model {state.model}</Text>
103 |         <Text color={theme.textDimmer}> · </Text>
104 |         <Text color={theme.accent}>quota {displayQuota} used</Text>
105 |         <Text color={theme.textDimmer}> · </Text>
106 |         <Text color={theme.accent}>context {displayContext} used</Text>
107 |         <Text color={theme.textDimmer}> · </Text>
108 |         <Text color={theme.textDim}>memory {state.memoryMB} MB</Text>
109 |         <Text color={theme.textDimmer}> · </Text>
110 |         <Text color={theme.textDim}>session {state.sessionId}</Text>
111 |         <Text color={theme.textDimmer}> · </Text>
112 |         <Text color={theme.text}>{displayTokens} tokens</Text>
113 |       </>
114 |     );
115 |   };
116 | 
117 |   return (
118 |     <Box width="100%" flexShrink={0}>
119 |       {getStatusContent()}
120 |     </Box>
121 |   );
122 | });
123 | 
124 | StatusBar.displayName = 'StatusBar';
125 | 
126 | // Made with Bob
</content>

<file><path>tui-ts/src/components/ChatInput.tsx</path>
<content lines="1-86">
 1 | import React, { useState } from 'react';
 2 | import { Box, Text } from 'ink';
 3 | import TextInput from 'ink-text-input';
 4 | import { CommandMenu } from './CommandMenu.js';
 5 | 
 6 | interface ChatInputProps {
 7 |   onSubmit: (value: string) => void;
 8 |   placeholder?: string;
 9 | }
10 | 
11 | export const ChatInput: React.FC<ChatInputProps> = ({
12 |   onSubmit,
13 |   placeholder = 'Type your message or @path/to/file',
14 | }) => {
15 |   const [value, setValue] = useState('');
16 |   const [showCommandMenu, setShowCommandMenu] = useState(false);
17 | 
18 |   const handleChange = (newValue: string) => {
19 |     setValue(newValue);
20 |     // Show command menu when "/" is typed
21 |     setShowCommandMenu(newValue.startsWith('/') && newValue.length > 0);
22 |   };
23 | 
24 |   const handleSubmit = () => {
25 |     if (value.trim()) {
26 |       onSubmit(value);
27 |       setValue('');
28 |       setShowCommandMenu(false);
29 |     }
30 |   };
31 | 
32 |   const handleMenuClose = () => {
33 |     setShowCommandMenu(false);
34 |   };
35 | 
36 |   return (
37 |     <Box flexDirection="column" width="100%">
38 |       {/* Separator Line */}
39 |       <Box width="100%" paddingX={1}>
40 |         <Text color="magenta">{'═'.repeat(80)}</Text>
41 |       </Box>
42 | 
43 |       {/* Input Box with improved styling */}
44 |       <Box flexDirection="column" paddingX={1} paddingY={1}>
45 |         {/* Label */}
46 |         <Box marginBottom={1}>
47 |           <Text color="gray">╭─ </Text>
48 |           <Text color="magenta" bold>prompt</Text>
49 |           <Text color="gray"> {'─'.repeat(70)}╮</Text>
50 |         </Box>
51 |         
52 |         {/* Input area */}
53 |         <Box paddingX={2}>
54 |           <Text color="magenta" bold>❯ </Text>
55 |           <TextInput
56 |             value={value}
57 |             onChange={handleChange}
58 |             onSubmit={handleSubmit}
59 |             placeholder={placeholder}
60 |           />
61 |         </Box>
62 |         
63 |         {/* Bottom border */}
64 |         <Box marginTop={1}>
65 |           <Text color="gray">╰{'─'.repeat(78)}╯</Text>
66 |         </Box>
67 |       </Box>
68 | 
69 |       {/* Command Menu - BELOW input, ABOVE status bar */}
70 |       {showCommandMenu && (
71 |         <Box marginTop={1} marginBottom={1}>
72 |           <CommandMenu
73 |             filter={value.slice(1)}
74 |             onSelect={(command) => {
75 |               setValue(command);
76 |               setShowCommandMenu(false);
77 |             }}
78 |             onClose={handleMenuClose}
79 |           />
80 |         </Box>
81 |       )}
82 |     </Box>
83 |   );
84 | };
85 | 
86 | // Made with Bob
</content>
</file>
<file><path>tui-ts/src/components/MessagesPanel.tsx</path>
<content lines="1-87">
 1 | import React from 'react';
 2 | import { Box, Text } from 'ink';
 3 | 
 4 | export interface Message {
 5 |   id: string;
 6 |   type: 'user' | 'assistant' | 'system';
 7 |   content: string;
 8 |   model?: string;
 9 |   timestamp: string;
10 | }
11 | 
12 | interface MessagesPanelProps {
13 |   messages: Message[];
14 | }
15 | 
16 | export const MessagesPanel: React.FC<MessagesPanelProps> = ({ messages }) => {
17 |   return (
18 |     <Box
19 |       flexDirection="column"
20 |       paddingX={1}
21 |       paddingY={1}
22 |       flexGrow={1}
23 |     >
24 |       {messages.length === 0 ? (
25 |         <Text color="gray">No messages yet. Start typing to begin...</Text>
26 |       ) : (
27 |         messages.map((message) => (
28 |           <Box key={message.id} flexDirection="column" marginBottom={1}>
29 |             {/* System messages with "!" prefix - inline style like Gemini */}
30 |             {message.type === 'system' && (
31 |               <Box>
32 |                 <Text color="yellow">! </Text>
33 |                 <Text color="white">{message.content}</Text>
34 |               </Box>
35 |             )}
36 | 
37 |             {/* User messages */}
38 |             {message.type === 'user' && (
39 |               <>
40 |                 <Box>
41 |                   <Text color="yellow">{'─'.repeat(80)}</Text>
42 |                 </Box>
43 |                 <Box marginTop={1}>
44 |                   <Text color="yellow">● </Text>
45 |                   <Text color="white" bold>User</Text>
46 |                 </Box>
47 |                 <Box
48 |                   borderStyle="single"
49 |                   borderColor="gray"
50 |                   paddingX={1}
51 |                   paddingY={0}
52 |                   marginTop={1}
53 |                 >
54 |                   <Text>{message.content}</Text>
55 |                 </Box>
56 |               </>
57 |             )}
58 | 
59 |             {/* Assistant messages */}
60 |             {message.type === 'assistant' && (
61 |               <>
62 |                 <Box>
63 |                   <Text color="yellow">{'─'.repeat(80)}</Text>
64 |                 </Box>
65 |                 <Box marginTop={1}>
66 |                   <Text color="yellow">▸ ⚡ </Text>
67 |                   <Text color="white" bold>{message.model || 'Elith'}</Text>
68 |                 </Box>
69 |                 <Box
70 |                   borderStyle="single"
71 |                   borderColor="gray"
72 |                   paddingX={1}
73 |                   paddingY={0}
74 |                   marginTop={1}
75 |                 >
76 |                   <Text>{message.content}</Text>
77 |                 </Box>
78 |               </>
79 |             )}
80 |           </Box>
81 |         ))
82 |       )}
83 |     </Box>
84 |   );
85 | };
86 | 
87 | // Made with Bob
</content>
</file>
<file><path>tui-ts/QUICK_START.md</path>
<content lines="1-134">
  1 | # Quick Start Guide - Elith TypeScript TUI
  2 | 
  3 | Get the new TypeScript TUI up and running in minutes.
  4 | 
  5 | ## Prerequisites
  6 | 
  7 | - Node.js 18+ installed
  8 | - Backend running on port 8000
  9 | 
 10 | ## Installation
 11 | 
 12 | ### Option 1: Global Installation (Recommended)
 13 | 
 14 | Install globally to run `elith-tui` from anywhere:
 15 | 
 16 | ```bash
 17 | cd tui-ts
 18 | ./install-global.sh
 19 | ```
 20 | 
 21 | After installation, run from any directory:
 22 | ```bash
 23 | elith-tui
 24 | ```
 25 | 
 26 | ### Option 2: Local Installation
 27 | 
 28 | ```bash
 29 | cd tui-ts
 30 | npm install
 31 | ```
 32 | 
 33 | ## Running
 34 | 
 35 | ### Global Command (After Global Install)
 36 | ```bash
 37 | elith-tui
 38 | ```
 39 | 
 40 | ### Development Mode
 41 | ```bash
 42 | cd tui-ts
 43 | npm run dev
 44 | ```
 45 | 
 46 | Or use the convenience script:
 47 | ```bash
 48 | ./run.sh
 49 | ```
 50 | 
 51 | ### Production Build
 52 | ```bash
 53 | npm run build
 54 | npm start
 55 | ```
 56 | 
 57 | ## First Steps
 58 | 
 59 | 1. **Start the backend** (in another terminal):
 60 |    ```bash
 61 |    python -m uvicorn backend.main:app --reload --port 8000
 62 |    ```
 63 | 
 64 | 2. **Run the TUI**:
 65 |    ```bash
 66 |    cd tui-ts
 67 |    npm run dev
 68 |    ```
 69 | 
 70 | 3. **Try these commands**:
 71 |    - `/help` - Show available commands
 72 |    - `/models` - List available AI models
 73 |    - `/model claude` - Switch to Claude
 74 |    - `Hello!` - Send a message to the AI
 75 | 
 76 | ## Available Commands
 77 | 
 78 | | Command | Description |
 79 | |---------|-------------|
 80 | | `/help` | Show help message |
 81 | | `/models` | List available models |
 82 | | `/model <name>` | Switch to a different model |
 83 | | `/clear` | Clear chat history |
 84 | | `/repo <path>` | Set repository path |
 85 | 
 86 | ## Keyboard Shortcuts
 87 | 
 88 | - `Ctrl+C` - Exit the application
 89 | - `Enter` - Submit message/command
 90 | 
 91 | ## Troubleshooting
 92 | 
 93 | ### "Backend not available"
 94 | Make sure the backend is running:
 95 | ```bash
 96 | python -m uvicorn backend.main:app --reload --port 8000
 97 | ```
 98 | 
 99 | ### TypeScript errors
100 | The TypeScript errors you see in the editor are expected before running `npm install`. They will be resolved once dependencies are installed.
101 | 
102 | ### Module not found
103 | Try reinstalling:
104 | ```bash
105 | rm -rf node_modules package-lock.json
106 | npm install
107 | ```
108 | 
109 | ## What's Different from Python TUI?
110 | 
111 | 1. **Same API** - Uses identical backend API
112 | 2. **Shared Code** - Types and API client shared with web frontend
113 | 3. **TypeScript** - Full type safety
114 | 4. **React** - Component-based architecture with Ink
115 | 5. **Modern** - npm ecosystem and tooling
116 | 
117 | ## Next Steps
118 | 
119 | - Explore the code in `src/`
120 | - Check out `README.md` for detailed documentation
121 | - Read `TUI_TYPESCRIPT_MIGRATION.md` for migration details
122 | - Add new features or screens
123 | 
124 | ## Support
125 | 
126 | For issues or questions:
127 | 1. Check the main README.md
128 | 2. Review TUI_TYPESCRIPT_MIGRATION.md
129 | 3. Inspect backend logs
130 | 4. Check browser console (for API errors)
131 | 
132 | ---
133 | 
134 | **Enjoy the new TypeScript TUI!** 🚀
</content>
</file>
</files>

<content lines="1-29">
 1 | #!/usr/bin/env node
 2 | 
 3 | import React from 'react';
 4 | import { render } from 'ink';
 5 | import { App } from './App.js';
 6 | 
 7 | // Clear terminal before rendering
 8 | process.stdout.write('\x1Bc');
 9 | 
10 | // Render the app
11 | const { unmount, waitUntilExit } = render(<App />);
12 | 
13 | // Handle cleanup
14 | process.on('SIGINT', () => {
15 |   unmount();
16 |   process.exit(0);
17 | });
18 | 
19 | process.on('SIGTERM', () => {
20 |   unmount();
21 |   process.exit(0);
22 | });
23 | 
24 | // Wait for exit
25 | waitUntilExit().then(() => {
26 |   process.exit(0);
27 | });
28 | 
29 | // Made with Bob
</content>
</file>

<file><path>README.md</path>
<content lines="1-100">
  1 | # Elith — Universal Repo-Aware AI Agent Framework
  2 | 
  3 | **IBM Bob Hackathon 2026**
  4 | 
  5 | Elith gives every AI model — Claude, Gemini, GPT, local LLMs — the same repo-awareness IBM Bob has natively, through an agent skill layer. Any model. Bob-level. Your choice.
  6 | 
  7 | ## What is Elith?
  8 | 
  9 | IBM Bob is powerful because it lives inside your repository — reading files, writing code, running tests natively. But most developers also use Claude, Gemini, GPT, or local LLMs, which are repo-blind by default.
 10 | 
 11 | **Elith fixes this** with a skill layer of 12 agent tools injected into any model via tool calling:
 12 | - **File Operations**: read_file, write_file, list_files
 13 | - **Code Analysis**: search_code, find_references, explain_function, analyze_dependencies
 14 | - **Git Operations**: git_diff, git_commit
 15 | - **Testing**: run_tests
 16 | - **Package Management**: install_package
 17 | - **Logging**: read_logs
 18 | 
 19 | ## Key Features
 20 | 
 21 | ✅ **Universal Model Support**: Works with Claude, Gemini, GPT, LMStudio, and any OpenAI-compatible API  
 22 | ✅ **Smart Context Engine**: Loads only 4-6 relevant files from 100+ (90%+ token reduction)  
 23 | ✅ **Obsidian Integration**: Project memory without vector DB overhead  
 24 | ✅ **Novel Architecture Generator**: Repo-specific proposals, not generic textbook answers  
 25 | ✅ **Dual Interface**: Terminal UI (Textual) + Web Dashboard (React)  
 26 | ✅ **Real-time Streaming**: SSE for live output in both interfaces
 27 | 
 28 | ## Quick Start
 29 | 
 30 | ### Prerequisites
 31 | - Python 3.10+
 32 | - Node.js 18+ (for frontend)
 33 | - Git
 34 | 
 35 | ### 1. Clone and Setup
 36 | 
 37 | ```bash
 38 | git clone https://github.com/yourusername/elith.git
 39 | cd elith
 40 | 
 41 | # Run automated setup
 42 | chmod +x install.sh
 43 | ./install.sh
 44 | ```
 45 | 
 46 | ### 2. Configure API Keys
 47 | 
 48 | Copy `.env.example` to `.env` and add your API keys:
 49 | 
 50 | ```bash
 51 | cp .env.example .env
 52 | ```
 53 | 
 54 | Edit `.env`:
 55 | ```env
 56 | # At least one provider required
 57 | ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=sk-ant-...           # For Claude
 58 | GOOGLE_API_KEY=AIza...                 # For Gemini
 59 | OPENAI_API_KEY=sk-...                  # For GPT-4
 60 | LMSTUDIO_BASE_URL=http://localhost:1234/v1  # For local LLMs
 61 | LMSTUDIO_API_KEY=lm-studio             # LMStudio API key
 62 | ```
 63 | 
 64 | ### 3. Run Backend
 65 | 
 66 | ```bash
 67 | source venv/bin/activate
 68 | python -m uvicorn backend.main:app --reload --port 8000
 69 | ```
 70 | 
 71 | Backend runs at: `http://localhost:8000`
 72 | 
 73 | ### 4. Run Terminal UI (Recommended)
 74 | 
 75 | ```bash
 76 | # In a new terminal
 77 | source venv/bin/activate
 78 | python -m tui.app
 79 | ```
 80 | 
 81 | ### 5. Run Web Dashboard (Optional)
 82 | 
 83 | ```bash
 84 | # In a new terminal
 85 | cd frontend
 86 | npm install
 87 | npm run dev
 88 | ```
 89 | 
 90 | Frontend runs at: `http://localhost:5173`
 91 | 
 92 | ## Usage
 93 | 
 94 | ### Terminal UI (TUI)
 95 | 
 96 | 1. Launch TUI: `python -m tui.app`
 97 | 2. Select workspace directory
 98 | 3. Choose operation: Explain, Architect, Test Gen, or Refactor
 99 | 4. Select AI model (only configured models shown)
100 | 5. Watch real-time output with context preview
</content>
</file>
</files>

  9 | import { Transcript } from './components/Transcript.js';
 10 | import { InputArea } from './components/InputArea.js';
 11 | import { CommandPanel } from './components/CommandPanel.js';
 12 | import { StatusBar } from './components/StatusBar.js';
 13 | import { createStreamConnection } from './api/stream.js';
 14 | import { api } from './api/client.js';
 15 | import { writeFileSync } from 'fs';
 16 | import type { AppState } from './types.js';
 17 | 
 18 | const AppContent: React.FC = () => {
 19 |   const dispatch = useAppDispatch();
 20 |   const state = useAppState();
 21 |   const [terminalSize, setTerminalSize] = React.useState({
 22 |     rows: process.stdout.rows || 24,
 23 |     cols: process.stdout.columns || 80,
 24 |   });
 25 | 
 26 |   // Handle terminal resize
 27 |   useEffect(() => {
 28 |     const handleResize = () => {
 29 |       setTerminalSize({
 30 |         rows: process.stdout.rows || 24,
 31 |         cols: process.stdout.columns || 80,
 32 |       });
 33 |     };
 34 | 
 35 |     process.stdout.on('resize', handleResize);
 36 |     return () => {
 37 |       process.stdout.off('resize', handleResize);
 38 |     };
 39 |   }, []);
 40 | 
 41 |   // Handle input submission
 42 |   const handleSubmit = async (value: string) => {
 43 |     // Dispatch user message
 44 |     dispatch({ type: 'USER_SUBMIT', payload: { text: value } });
 45 | 
 46 |     // Handle commands
 47 |     if (value.startsWith('/')) {
 48 |       handleCommand(value);
 49 |       return;
 50 |     }
 51 | 
 52 |     // Execute task
 53 |     try {
 54 |       const response = await api.execute({
 55 |         model: state.model,
 56 |         operation: 'explain',
 57 |         repo_path: state.workspace,
 58 |         prompt: value,
 59 |       });
 60 | 
 61 |       // Connect to stream
 62 |       const cleanup = createStreamConnection(
 63 |         response.session_id,
 64 |         (event) => {
 65 |           switch (event.type) {
 66 |             case 'thinking':
 67 |               dispatch({ type: 'THINKING_CHUNK', payload: { chunk: event.content || '' } });
 68 |               break;
 69 |             case 'output':
 70 |               dispatch({ type: 'STREAM_CHUNK', payload: { chunk: event.content || '' } });
 71 |               break;
 72 |             case 'tool':
 73 |               if (event.tool) {
 74 |                 dispatch({ type: 'TOOL_START', payload: event.tool });
 75 |               }
 76 |               break;
 77 |             case 'subagent':
 78 |               if (event.subagent) {
 79 |                 dispatch({
 80 |                   type: 'SUBAGENT_START',
 81 |                   payload: { id: event.subagent.id, label: event.subagent.label },
 82 |                 });
 83 |               }
 84 |               break;
 85 |             case 'done':
 86 |               dispatch({ type: 'STREAM_DONE' });
 87 |               break;
 88 |             case 'error':
 89 |               dispatch({ type: 'STREAM_ERROR', payload: { error: event.error || 'Unknown error' } });
 90 |               break;
 91 |             case 'approval':
 92 |               dispatch({ type: 'AWAITING_APPROVAL' });
 93 |               break;
 94 |           }
 95 |         },
 96 |         (error) => {
 97 |           dispatch({ type: 'STREAM_ERROR', payload: { error: error.message } });
 98 |         }
 99 |       );
100 | 
101 |       return cleanup;
102 |     } catch (error) {
103 |       dispatch({
104 |         type: 'STREAM_ERROR',
105 |         payload: { error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error' },
106 |       });
107 |     }
108 |   };
109 | 
110 |   const handleCommand = (command: string) => {
111 |     const cmd = command.toLowerCase();
112 |     const parts = command.split(' ');
113 |     
114 |     if (cmd === '/help') {
115 |       dispatch({
116 |         type: 'STREAM_CHUNK',
117 |         payload: {
118 |           chunk: `Available commands:
119 | - /help - Show this help
120 | - /clear - Clear transcript
121 | - /model [name] - Show/switch model (claude, lmstudio, openrouter)
122 | - /skills - List skills
123 | - /context - Show context files
124 | - /auth - Show backend connection status
125 | - /mode - Toggle autonomous ↔ confirm mode
126 | - /scan - Re-scan workspace files
127 | - /export - Save session to markdown
128 | - /exit - Quit`,
129 |         },
130 |       });
131 |       dispatch({ type: 'STREAM_DONE' });
132 |     } else if (cmd === '/clear') {
133 |       dispatch({ type: 'CLEAR_TRANSCRIPT' });
134 |     } else if (cmd.startsWith('/model')) {
135 |       const targetModel = parts[1];
136 |       const validModels = ['claude', 'lmstudio', 'openrouter'];
137 |       
138 |       if (!targetModel) {
139 |         dispatch({
140 |           type: 'STREAM_CHUNK',
141 |           payload: {
142 |             chunk: `Current model: ${state.model}\n\nAvailable models:\n- claude\n- lmstudio\n- openrouter\n\nUsage: /model <name>`,
143 |           },
144 |         });
145 |         dispatch({ type: 'STREAM_DONE' });
146 |       } else if (validModels.includes(targetModel)) {
147 |         dispatch({ type: 'SET_MODEL', payload: targetModel });
148 |         dispatch({
149 |           type: 'STREAM_CHUNK',
150 |           payload: { chunk: `Switched to model: ${targetModel}` },
151 |         });
152 |         dispatch({ type: 'STREAM_DONE' });
153 |       } else {
154 |         dispatch({
155 |           type: 'STREAM_CHUNK',
156 |           payload: {
157 |             chunk: `Unknown model: ${targetModel}. Valid: claude, lmstudio, openrouter`,
158 |           },
159 |         });
160 |         dispatch({ type: 'STREAM_DONE' });
161 |       }
162 |     } else if (cmd === '/skills') {
163 |       const skillsList = [
164 |         'read_file',
165 |         'write_file',
166 |         'list_files',
167 |         'search_code',
168 |         'git_diff',
169 |         'git_commit',
170 |         'run_tests',
171 |         'find_references',
172 |         'analyze_dependencies',
173 |         'explain_function',
174 |         'install_package',
175 |         'read_logs',
176 |       ];
177 |       const output = `12 skills active:\n\n${skillsList.map(s => `  ⚡ ${s}`).join('\n')}`;
178 |       dispatch({ type: 'STREAM_CHUNK', payload: { chunk: output } });
179 |       dispatch({ type: 'STREAM_DONE' });
180 |     } else if (cmd === '/auth') {
181 |       const isOnline = state.backendStatus === 'online';
182 |       const output = isOnline
183 |         ? `Backend: ✓ online (http://localhost:8000)\nModel: ${state.model}\nSkills: 12 active`
184 |         : `Backend: ✗ offline\n\nStart the backend:\n  source venv/bin/activate\n  python -m uvicorn backend.main:app --reload --port 8000`;
185 |       dispatch({ type: 'STREAM_CHUNK', payload: { chunk: output } });
186 |       dispatch({ type: 'STREAM_DONE' });
187 |     } else if (cmd === '/mode') {
188 |       const newMode = state.mode === 'autonomous' ? 'confirm' : 'autonomous';
189 |       dispatch({ type: 'SET_MODE', payload: newMode });
190 |       dispatch({
191 |         type: 'STREAM_CHUNK',
192 |         payload: { chunk: `Mode: ${newMode}` },
193 |       });
194 |       dispatch({ type: 'STREAM_DONE' });
195 |     } else if (cmd === '/scan') {
196 |       dispatch({
197 |         type: 'STREAM_CHUNK',
198 |         payload: { chunk: `Scanning ${state.workspace}...` },
199 |       });
200 |       dispatch({ type: 'SCAN_WORKSPACE' });
201 |       dispatch({ type: 'STREAM_CHUNK', payload: { chunk: `Done.` } });
202 |       dispatch({ type: 'STREAM_DONE' });
203 |     } else if (cmd === '/export') {
204 |       const timestamp = new Date()
205 |         .toISOString()
206 |         .replace(/[:.]/g, '-')
207 |         .slice(0, 19);
208 |       const filename = `elith-session-${timestamp}.md`;
209 |       const content = buildSessionMarkdown(state);
210 |       try {
211 |         writeFileSync(filename, content);
212 |         dispatch({
213 |           type: 'STREAM_CHUNK',
214 |           payload: { chunk: `Session saved to: ${filename}` },
215 |         });
216 |       } catch (error) {
217 |         dispatch({
218 |           type: 'STREAM_CHUNK',
219 |           payload: {
220 |             chunk: `Error saving session: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error'}`,
221 |           },
222 |         });
223 |       }
224 |       dispatch({ type: 'STREAM_DONE' });
225 |     } else if (cmd === '/exit') {
226 |       process.exit(0);
227 |     } else {
228 |       dispatch({
229 |         type: 'STREAM_CHUNK',
230 |         payload: { chunk: `Unknown command: ${command}. Type /help for available commands.` },
231 |       });
232 |       dispatch({ type: 'STREAM_DONE' });
233 |     }
234 |   };
235 | 
236 |   const buildSessionMarkdown = (appState: AppState): string => {
237 |     const date = new Date().toLocaleString();
238 |     const lines = [
239 |       `# Elith Session — ${date}`,
240 |       ``,
241 |       `**Workspace:** ${appState.workspace}`,
242 |       `**Model:** ${appState.model}`,
243 |       `**Branch:** ${appState.branch}`,
244 |       ``,
245 |       `---`,
246 |       ``,
247 |     ];
248 | 
249 |     for (const msg of appState.messages) {
250 |       if (msg.role === 'user') {
251 |         lines.push(`**You:** ${msg.text}`, ``);
252 |       } else {
253 |         lines.push(`**Elith:** ${msg.text}`, ``);
254 |       }
255 |       lines.push(`---`, ``);
256 |     }
257 | 
258 |     return lines.join('\n');
259 |   };
260 | 
261 |   // Setup keyboard input
262 |   useKeyboardInput({
263 |     onSubmit: handleSubmit,
264 |     onCancel: () => process.exit(0),
265 |   });
266 | 
267 |   // Poll for status updates
268 |   useEffect(() => {
269 |     const poll = async () => {
270 |       try {
271 |         const status = await api.getStatus();
272 |         dispatch({
273 |           type: 'UPDATE_STATS',
274 |           payload: {
275 |             ctxPercent: status.ctx_percent ?? 0,
276 |             quotaPercent: status.quota_percent ?? 0,
277 |             memoryMB: status.memory_mb ?? 0,
278 |             tokens: status.tokens ?? 0,
279 |           },
280 |         });
281 |         dispatch({ type: 'SET_BACKEND_STATUS', payload: 'online' });
282 |       } catch {
283 |         // Silently fail - don't log to console, just mark offline
284 |         dispatch({ type: 'SET_BACKEND_STATUS', payload: 'offline' });
285 |       }
286 |     };
287 | 
288 |     poll(); // immediate first check
289 |     const interval = setInterval(poll, 5000);
290 |     return () => clearInterval(interval);
291 |   }, [dispatch]);
292 | 
293 |   return (
294 |     <Box flexDirection="column" width="100%" height="100%">
295 |       {/* 1. BANNER - Compact, fixed at top */}
296 |       <Box flexShrink={0}>
297 |         <Banner />
298 |       </Box>
299 | 
300 |       {/* 2. TRANSCRIPT - Scrollable, takes all remaining space */}
301 |       <Box flexGrow={1} flexShrink={1} minHeight={0}>
302 |         <Transcript />
303 |       </Box>
304 | 
305 |       {/* 3. INPUT AREA - Fixed at bottom */}
306 |       <Box flexShrink={0}>
307 |         <InputArea onSubmit={handleSubmit} />
308 |       </Box>
309 | 
310 |       {/* 4. COMMAND PANEL - Shown only when trigger active */}
311 |       {state.triggerMode && (
312 |         <Box flexShrink={0}>
313 |           <CommandPanel />
314 |         </Box>
315 |       )}
316 | 
317 |       {/* 5. STATUS BAR - Fixed at bottom */}
318 |       <Box flexShrink={0}>
319 |         <StatusBar />
320 |       </Box>
321 |     </Box>
322 |   );
323 | };
324 | 
325 | export const App: React.FC = () => {
326 |   return (
327 |     <AppStoreProvider>
328 |       <AppContent />
329 |     </AppStoreProvider>
330 |   );
331 | };
332 | 
333 | // Made with Bob
</content>
</file>
<file><path>tui-ts/src/store/appStore.tsx</path>
<content lines="1-401">
  1 | // Global state management with useReducer + Context
  2 | 
  3 | import React, { createContext, useContext, useReducer, type Dispatch } from 'react';
  4 | import type { AppState, AppAction, Message, ExpandableBlock } from '../types.js';
  5 | import { execSync } from 'child_process';
  6 | 
  7 | // Initial state
  8 | const initialState: AppState = {
  9 |   messages: [],
 10 |   status: 'idle',
 11 |   model: 'lmstudio',
 12 |   branch: getBranch(),
 13 |   workspace: process.cwd(),
 14 |   sandbox: 'no sandbox',
 15 |   ctxPercent: 0,
 16 |   quotaPercent: 0,
 17 |   memoryMB: getMemoryUsage(),
 18 |   tokens: 0,
 19 |   sessionId: generateSessionId(),
 20 |   inputHistory: [],
 21 |   historyIndex: -1,
 22 |   queuedInput: null,
 23 |   autoScroll: true,
 24 |   triggerMode: null,
 25 |   triggerQuery: '',
 26 |   panelSelectedIndex: 0,
 27 |   focusedExpandableId: null,
 28 |   mode: 'autonomous',
 29 |   backendStatus: 'unknown',
 30 | };
 31 | 
 32 | // Helper functions
 33 | function getBranch(): string {
 34 |   try {
 35 |     return execSync('git branch --show-current', { encoding: 'utf-8' }).trim();
 36 |   } catch {
 37 |     return 'main';
 38 |   }
 39 | }
 40 | 
 41 | function getMemoryUsage(): number {
 42 |   return Math.round(process.memoryUsage().heapUsed / 1024 / 1024 * 10) / 10;
 43 | }
 44 | 
 45 | function generateSessionId(): string {
 46 |   return Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 10);
 47 | }
 48 | 
 49 | // Reducer
 50 | export function appReducer(state: AppState, action: AppAction): AppState {
 51 |   switch (action.type) {
 52 |     case 'USER_SUBMIT': {
 53 |       const newMessage: Message = {
 54 |         id: Date.now().toString(),
 55 |         role: 'user',
 56 |         text: action.payload.text,
 57 |         timestamp: Date.now(),
 58 |       };
 59 |       return {
 60 |         ...state,
 61 |         messages: [...state.messages, newMessage],
 62 |         status: 'thinking',
 63 |         inputHistory: [...state.inputHistory, action.payload.text],
 64 |         historyIndex: -1,
 65 |         queuedInput: null,
 66 |       };
 67 |     }
 68 | 
 69 |     case 'THINKING_CHUNK': {
 70 |       const messages = [...state.messages];
 71 |       const lastMessage = messages[messages.length - 1];
 72 |       
 73 |       if (!lastMessage) return state;
 74 | 
 75 |       if (!lastMessage.thinking) {
 76 |         lastMessage.thinking = {
 77 |           id: `thinking-${Date.now()}`,
 78 |           type: 'thinking',
 79 |           label: 'Thinking',
 80 |           body: action.payload.chunk,
 81 |           collapsed: false,
 82 |           active: true,
 83 |           focused: false,
 84 |         };
 85 |       } else {
 86 |         lastMessage.thinking.body += action.payload.chunk;
 87 |       }
 88 | 
 89 |       return { ...state, messages };
 90 |     }
 91 | 
 92 |     case 'THINKING_DONE': {
 93 |       const messages = [...state.messages];
 94 |       const lastMessage = messages[messages.length - 1];
 95 |       
 96 |       if (lastMessage?.thinking) {
 97 |         lastMessage.thinking.active = false;
 98 |         lastMessage.thinking.durationMs = Date.now() - parseInt(lastMessage.thinking.id.split('-')[1]);
 99 |       }
100 | 
101 |       return { ...state, messages };
102 |     }
103 | 
104 |     case 'SUBAGENT_START': {
105 |       const messages = [...state.messages];
106 |       const lastMessage = messages[messages.length - 1];
107 |       
108 |       if (!lastMessage) return state;
109 | 
110 |       const newSubAgent: ExpandableBlock = {
111 |         id: action.payload.id,
112 |         type: 'subagent',
113 |         label: action.payload.label,
114 |         body: '',
115 |         collapsed: true,
116 |         active: true,
117 |         focused: false,
118 |       };
119 | 
120 |       if (!lastMessage.subAgents) {
121 |         lastMessage.subAgents = [];
122 |       }
123 |       lastMessage.subAgents.push(newSubAgent);
124 | 
125 |       return { ...state, messages };
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     case 'SUBAGENT_CHUNK': {
129 |       const messages = [...state.messages];
130 |       const lastMessage = messages[messages.length - 1];
131 |       
132 |       if (!lastMessage?.subAgents) return state;
133 | 
134 |       const subAgent = lastMessage.subAgents.find(sa => sa.id === action.payload.id);
135 |       if (subAgent) {
136 |         subAgent.body += action.payload.chunk;
137 |       }
138 | 
139 |       return { ...state, messages };
140 |     }
141 | 
142 |     case 'SUBAGENT_DONE': {
143 |       const messages = [...state.messages];
144 |       const lastMessage = messages[messages.length - 1];
145 |       
146 |       if (!lastMessage?.subAgents) return state;
147 | 
148 |       const subAgent = lastMessage.subAgents.find(sa => sa.id === action.payload.id);
149 |       if (subAgent) {
150 |         subAgent.active = false;
151 |         subAgent.durationMs = Date.now() - parseInt(subAgent.id.split('-')[1] || '0');
152 |       }
153 | 
154 |       return { ...state, messages };
155 |     }
156 | 
157 |     case 'STREAM_CHUNK': {
158 |       const messages = [...state.messages];
159 |       let lastMessage = messages[messages.length - 1];
160 |       
161 |       // If last message is not from agent, create new agent message
162 |       if (!lastMessage || lastMessage.role !== 'agent') {
163 |         lastMessage = {
164 |           id: Date.now().toString(),
165 |           role: 'agent',
166 |           text: action.payload.chunk,
167 |           timestamp: Date.now(),
168 |         };
169 |         messages.push(lastMessage);
170 |       } else {
171 |         lastMessage.text += action.payload.chunk;
172 |       }
173 | 
174 |       return { ...state, messages, status: 'streaming' };
175 |     }
176 | 
177 |     case 'TOOL_START': {
178 |       const messages = [...state.messages];
179 |       const lastMessage = messages[messages.length - 1];
180 |       
181 |       if (!lastMessage) return state;
182 | 
183 |       if (!lastMessage.tools) {
184 |         lastMessage.tools = [];
185 |       }
186 |       lastMessage.tools.push(action.payload);
187 | 
188 |       return { ...state, messages, status: 'tool_running' };
189 |     }
190 | 
191 |     case 'TOOL_DONE': {
192 |       const messages = [...state.messages];
193 |       const lastMessage = messages[messages.length - 1];
194 |       
195 |       if (!lastMessage?.tools) return state;
196 | 
197 |       const tool = lastMessage.tools.find(t => t.id === action.payload.id);
198 |       if (tool) {
199 |         tool.status = action.payload.status;
200 |         tool.result = action.payload.result;
201 |       }
202 | 
203 |       return { ...state, messages };
204 |     }
205 | 
206 |     case 'ACTIVITY_LOG': {
207 |       const messages = [...state.messages];
208 |       const lastMessage = messages[messages.length - 1];
209 |       
210 |       if (!lastMessage) return state;
211 | 
212 |       if (!lastMessage.activityLogs) {
213 |         lastMessage.activityLogs = [];
214 |       }
215 |       lastMessage.activityLogs.push(action.payload.message);
216 |       
217 |       // Keep only last 5 activity logs
218 |       if (lastMessage.activityLogs.length > 5) {
219 |         lastMessage.activityLogs.shift();
220 |       }
221 | 
222 |       return { ...state, messages };
223 |     }
224 | 
225 |     case 'STREAM_DONE': {
226 |       return { ...state, status: 'idle' };
227 |     }
228 | 
229 |     case 'STREAM_ERROR': {
230 |       const errorMessage: Message = {
231 |         id: Date.now().toString(),
232 |         role: 'system',
233 |         text: `Error: ${action.payload.error}`,
234 |         timestamp: Date.now(),
235 |       };
236 |       return {
237 |         ...state,
238 |         messages: [...state.messages, errorMessage],
239 |         status: 'idle',
240 |       };
241 |     }
242 | 
243 |     case 'AWAITING_APPROVAL': {
244 |       const messages = [...state.messages];
245 |       const lastMessage = messages[messages.length - 1];
246 |       
247 |       if (lastMessage) {
248 |         lastMessage.awaitingApproval = true;
249 |       }
250 | 
251 |       return { ...state, messages, status: 'awaiting_approval' };
252 |     }
253 | 
254 |     case 'APPROVAL_RESULT': {
255 |       const messages = [...state.messages];
256 |       const lastMessage = messages[messages.length - 1];
257 |       
258 |       if (lastMessage) {
259 |         lastMessage.awaitingApproval = false;
260 |       }
261 | 
262 |       return { ...state, messages, status: 'idle' };
263 |     }
264 | 
265 |     case 'CLEAR_TRANSCRIPT': {
266 |       return { ...state, messages: [] };
267 |     }
268 | 
269 |     case 'SET_MODEL': {
270 |       return { ...state, model: action.payload };
271 |     }
272 | 
273 |     case 'SET_MODE': {
274 |       return { ...state, mode: action.payload };
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     case 'SET_BACKEND_STATUS': {
278 |       return { ...state, backendStatus: action.payload };
279 |     }
280 | 
281 |     case 'SCAN_WORKSPACE': {
282 |       // Trigger workspace rescan - currently just returns state
283 |       // Could be extended to actually rescan files
284 |       return state;
285 |     }
286 | 
287 |     case 'SET_AUTO_SCROLL': {
288 |       return { ...state, autoScroll: action.payload.autoScroll };
289 |     }
290 | 
291 |     case 'SET_TRIGGER': {
292 |       return {
293 |         ...state,
294 |         triggerMode: action.payload.mode,
295 |         triggerQuery: action.payload.query,
296 |         panelSelectedIndex: 0,
297 |       };
298 |     }
299 | 
300 |     case 'CLOSE_TRIGGER': {
301 |       return {
302 |         ...state,
303 |         triggerMode: null,
304 |         triggerQuery: '',
305 |         panelSelectedIndex: 0,
306 |       };
307 |     }
308 | 
309 |     case 'PANEL_NAVIGATE': {
310 |       const delta = action.payload.direction === 'up' ? -1 : 1;
311 |       const newIndex = Math.max(0, state.panelSelectedIndex + delta);
312 |       return { ...state, panelSelectedIndex: newIndex };
313 |     }
314 | 
315 |     case 'SET_FOCUS_EXPANDABLE': {
316 |       return { ...state, focusedExpandableId: action.payload.id };
317 |     }
318 | 
319 |     case 'TOGGLE_EXPANDABLE': {
320 |       const messages = state.messages.map(msg => {
321 |         // Toggle thinking block
322 |         if (msg.thinking?.id === action.payload.id) {
323 |           return {
324 |             ...msg,
325 |             thinking: {
326 |               ...msg.thinking,
327 |               collapsed: !msg.thinking.collapsed,
328 |             },
329 |           };
330 |         }
331 |         
332 |         // Toggle subagent block
333 |         if (msg.subAgents) {
334 |           return {
335 |             ...msg,
336 |             subAgents: msg.subAgents.map(sa =>
337 |               sa.id === action.payload.id
338 |                 ? { ...sa, collapsed: !sa.collapsed }
339 |                 : sa
340 |             ),
341 |           };
342 |         }
343 |         
344 |         return msg;
345 |       });
346 | 
347 |       return { ...state, messages };
348 |     }
349 | 
350 |     case 'UPDATE_STATUS': {
351 |       return { ...state, status: action.payload.status };
352 |     }
353 | 
354 |     case 'UPDATE_STATS': {
355 |       return {
356 |         ...state,
357 |         ...action.payload,
358 |         memoryMB: getMemoryUsage(),
359 |       };
360 |     }
361 | 
362 |     default:
363 |       return state;
364 |   }
365 | }
366 | 
367 | // Context
368 | const AppStateContext = createContext<AppState | undefined>(undefined);
369 | const AppDispatchContext = createContext<Dispatch<AppAction> | undefined>(undefined);
370 | 
371 | // Provider
372 | export function AppStoreProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
373 |   const [state, dispatch] = useReducer(appReducer, initialState);
374 | 
375 |   return (
376 |     <AppStateContext.Provider value={state}>
377 |       <AppDispatchContext.Provider value={dispatch}>
378 |         {children}
379 |       </AppDispatchContext.Provider>
380 |     </AppStateContext.Provider>
381 |   );
382 | }
383 | 
384 | // Hooks
385 | export function useAppState(): AppState {
386 |   const context = useContext(AppStateContext);
387 |   if (context === undefined) {
388 |     throw new Error('useAppState must be used within AppStoreProvider');
389 |   }
390 |   return context;
391 | }
392 | 
393 | export function useAppDispatch(): Dispatch<AppAction> {
394 |   const context = useContext(AppDispatchContext);
395 |   if (context === undefined) {
396 |     throw new Error('useAppDispatch must be used within AppStoreProvider');
397 |   }
398 |   return context;
399 | }
400 | 
401 | // Made with Bob
</content>
</file>

 8 |   show?: boolean;

10 |

12 |   message,

23 |     processing: '◑',

29 |     analyzing: 'yellow',
30 |     thinking: 'blue',
31 |     processing: 'green',
32 |   } as const;

35 |     <Box paddingX={1} marginY={0}>
36 |       <Text color={colors[type]}>
37 |         {type === 'analyzing' ? icons[type] : <Spinner type="dots" />}
38 |       </Text>

40 |     </Box>
41 |   );
42 | };

<file><path>tui-ts/src/components/CommandPanel.tsx</path>
<content lines="1-129">
  1 | // Command panel - overlay for /, @, !, # triggers
  2 | 
  3 | import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
  4 | import { Box, Text } from 'ink';
  5 | import { useAppState, useAppDispatch } from '../store/appStore.js';
  6 | import { theme, glyphs } from '../theme.js';
  7 | import type { CommandItem, FileItem } from '../types.js';
  8 | import { readdirSync, statSync } from 'fs';
  9 | import { join, relative } from 'path';
 10 | 
 11 | const SLASH_COMMANDS: CommandItem[] = [
 12 |   { id: 'help', icon: '?', name: '/help', description: 'List all commands and keybindings' },
 13 |   { id: 'clear', icon: '✗', name: '/clear', description: 'Clear transcript history' },
 14 |   { id: 'model', icon: '◆', name: '/model', description: 'Show and switch active model' },
 15 |   { id: 'skills', icon: '⚡', name: '/skills', description: 'List loaded agent skills' },
 16 |   { id: 'context', icon: '#', name: '/context', description: 'Show pinned @context files' },
 17 |   { id: 'history', icon: '⟳', name: '/history', description: 'Browse past sessions' },
 18 |   { id: 'auth', icon: '🔑', name: '/auth', description: 'Show backend connection status' },
 19 |   { id: 'mode', icon: '⚙', name: '/mode', description: 'Toggle autonomous ↔ confirm mode' },
 20 |   { id: 'scan', icon: '🔍', name: '/scan', description: 'Re-scan workspace files' },
 21 |   { id: 'export', icon: '💾', name: '/export', description: 'Save session to markdown file' },
 22 |   { id: 'exit', icon: '⏻', name: '/exit', description: 'Quit Elith' },
 23 | ];
 24 | 
 25 | function scanLocalFiles(dir: string, depth = 0): FileItem[] {
 26 |   if (depth > 3) return [];
 27 |   const SKIP = new Set(['.git', 'node_modules', '__pycache__', 'venv', '.venv', 'dist', 'build']);
 28 |   try {
 29 |     return readdirSync(dir).flatMap(name => {
 30 |       if (SKIP.has(name)) return [];
 31 |       const full = join(dir, name);
 32 |       const stat = statSync(full);
 33 |       const path = relative(process.cwd(), full);
 34 |       if (stat.isDirectory()) {
 35 |         return [{ path, type: 'directory' as const }, ...scanLocalFiles(full, depth + 1)];
 36 |       }
 37 |       return [{ path, type: 'file' as const }];
 38 |     });
 39 |   } catch {
 40 |     return [];
 41 |   }
 42 | }
 43 | 
 44 | export const CommandPanel: React.FC = () => {
 45 |   const state = useAppState();
 46 |   const dispatch = useAppDispatch();
 47 |   const [files, setFiles] = useState<FileItem[]>([]);
 48 | 
 49 |   // Scan files locally for @ trigger
 50 |   useEffect(() => {
 51 |     if (state.triggerMode === 'file') {
 52 |       const scannedFiles = scanLocalFiles(state.workspace);
 53 |       setFiles(scannedFiles);
 54 |     }
 55 |   }, [state.triggerMode, state.workspace]);
 56 | 
 57 |   if (!state.triggerMode) return null;
 58 | 
 59 |   const getItems = (): CommandItem[] => {
 60 |     switch (state.triggerMode) {
 61 |       case 'slash':
 62 |         return SLASH_COMMANDS.filter(cmd =>
 63 |           cmd.name.toLowerCase().includes(state.triggerQuery.toLowerCase())
 64 |         );
 65 |       case 'file':
 66 |         return files
 67 |           .filter(f => f.path.toLowerCase().includes(state.triggerQuery.toLowerCase()))
 68 |           .slice(0, 10)
 69 |           .map(f => ({
 70 |             id: f.path,
 71 |             icon: f.type === 'directory' ? '📁' : '📄',
 72 |             name: f.path,
 73 |             description: f.type,
 74 |           }));
 75 |       case 'shell':
 76 |         return [
 77 |           { id: 'shell', icon: '⚡', name: 'Shell passthrough', description: 'command runs directly' },
 78 |         ];
 79 |       case 'context':
 80 |         return files
 81 |           .filter(f => f.type === 'file' && f.path.toLowerCase().includes(state.triggerQuery.toLowerCase()))
 82 |           .slice(0, 10)
 83 |           .map(f => ({
 84 |             id: f.path,
 85 |             icon: '#',
 86 |             name: f.path,
 87 |             description: '[add to context]',
 88 |           }));
 89 |       default:
 90 |         return [];
 91 |     }
 92 |   };
 93 | 
 94 |   const items = getItems();
 95 |   const maxRows = 4;
 96 |   const visibleItems = items.slice(0, maxRows);
 97 | 
 98 |   return (
 99 |     <Box
100 |       flexDirection="column"
101 |       borderStyle="single"
102 |       borderColor={theme.borderDim}
103 |       width="100%"
104 |       paddingX={1}
105 |     >
106 |       {visibleItems.length === 0 ? (
107 |         <Text color={theme.textDim}>No matches found</Text>
108 |       ) : (
109 |         visibleItems.map((item, index) => {
110 |           const isSelected = index === state.panelSelectedIndex;
111 |           return (
112 |             <Box
113 |               key={item.id}
114 |               backgroundColor={isSelected ? theme.surfaceAlt : undefined}
115 |             >
116 |               <Text color={theme.accent}>{item.icon} </Text>
117 |               <Text color={isSelected ? theme.text : theme.textDim} bold={isSelected}>
118 |                 {item.name}
119 |               </Text>
120 |               <Text color={theme.textDim}> · {item.description}</Text>
121 |             </Box>
122 |           );
123 |         })
124 |       )}
125 |     </Box>
126 |   );
127 | };
128 | 
129 | // Made with Bob
</content>
</file>
</files>

<content>
# 🚀 Superb TUI Enhancement Plan - Elith

## Vision
Transform Elith's TUI into a **world-class terminal interface** with modern aesthetics, smooth animations, advanced features, and exceptional UX inspired by the best TUIs: GitHub CLI, Vercel CLI, Warp Terminal, and modern IDEs.

---

## 🎯 Core Enhancements

### 1. **Visual Excellence**
- ✨ **Smooth Animations**: Fade-in/out, slide transitions, progress bars
- 🎨 **Multiple Themes**: Dark (default), Light, Cyberpunk, Dracula, Nord, Solarized
- 🌈 **Syntax Highlighting**: Full code highlighting with Prism.js integration
- 💫 **Particle Effects**: Subtle background animations for thinking states
- 🎭 **Glassmorphism**: Frosted glass effects for panels and overlays

### 2. **Advanced Layout System**
- 📱 **Split Panes**: Horizontal/vertical splits (Ctrl+\, Ctrl+-)
- 📑 **Tabs System**: Multiple chat sessions (Ctrl+T new, Ctrl+W close)
- 🗂️ **File Tree Sidebar**: Collapsible file explorer (Ctrl+B toggle)
- 📊 **Dashboard View**: Stats, graphs, token usage visualization
- 🔄 **Layout Presets**: Code Review, Chat, Debug, Architect modes

### 3. **Enhanced Interactions**
- ⌨️ **Vim Keybindings**: Optional vim-style navigation
- 🖱️ **Mouse Support**: Click, scroll, drag-to-resize panes
- 🔍 **Fuzzy Search**: Ctrl+P for files, Ctrl+Shift+P for commands
- 📋 **Clipboard Integration**: Copy/paste with system clipboard
- ⏮️ **Undo/Redo**: Ctrl+Z/Ctrl+Y for input history

### 4. **Smart Features**
- 🤖 **AI Autocomplete**: Inline suggestions as you type
- 📝 **Rich Markdown**: Tables, checkboxes, mermaid diagrams
- 🔗 **Hyperlinks**: Clickable file paths, URLs, git commits
- 🎯 **Context Menu**: Right-click for actions
- 💡 **Tooltips**: Hover hints for commands and features

### 5. **Performance & Polish**
- ⚡ **Virtual Scrolling**: Handle 10,000+ messages smoothly
- 🎬 **60 FPS Animations**: Buttery smooth transitions
- 💾 **Session Persistence**: Auto-save and restore sessions
- 🔔 **Notifications**: Desktop notifications for long tasks
- 📸 **Screenshots**: Export TUI as PNG/SVG

---

## 🏗️ Architecture Improvements

### Component Structure
```
tui-ts/src/
├── components/
│   ├── layout/
│   │   ├── SplitPane.tsx          # Resizable split panes
│   │   ├── TabBar.tsx             # Multi-tab management
│   │   ├── Sidebar.tsx            # File tree sidebar
│   │   └── Dashboard.tsx          # Stats dashboard
│   ├── editor/
│   │   ├── CodeEditor.tsx         # Inline code editing
│   │   ├── DiffViewer.tsx         # Enhanced diff view
│   │   └── SyntaxHighlight.tsx    # Code highlighting
│   ├── ui/
│   │   ├── Modal.tsx              # Modal dialogs
│   │   ├── ContextMenu.tsx        # Right-click menu
│   │   ├── Tooltip.tsx            # Hover tooltips
│   │   ├── ProgressBar.tsx        # Animated progress
│   │   └── Notification.tsx       # Toast notifications
│   └── animations/
│       ├── FadeIn.tsx             # Fade animations
│       ├── SlideIn.tsx            # Slide animations
│       └── ParticleField.tsx      # Background effects
├── themes/
│   ├── dark.ts                    # Default dark theme
│   ├── light.ts                   # Light theme
│   ├── cyberpunk.ts               # Neon cyberpunk
│   ├── dracula.ts                 # Dracula theme
│   └── nord.ts                    # Nord theme
├── hooks/
│   ├── useTheme.ts                # Theme management
│   ├── useKeyboard.ts             # Advanced keyboard
│   ├── useMouse.ts                # Mouse interactions
│   ├── useClipboard.ts            # Clipboard ops
│   └── useVirtualScroll.ts        # Virtual scrolling
└── utils/
    ├── syntax.ts                  # Syntax highlighting
    ├── markdown.ts                # Markdown rendering
    └── animations.ts              # Animation helpers
```

---

## 🎨 New Features Implementation

### Feature 1: Split Panes
```typescript
// Usage: Ctrl+\ for vertical split, Ctrl+- for horizontal
<SplitPane orientation="vertical" sizes={[60, 40]}>
  <ChatPanel />
  <FilePreview />
</SplitPane>
```

### Feature 2: Tabs System
```typescript
// Multiple chat sessions with tabs
<TabBar>
  <Tab id="chat-1" title="Main Chat" active />
  <Tab id="chat-2" title="Code Review" />
  <Tab id="chat-3" title="Debug Session" />
</TabBar>
```

### Feature 3: File Tree Sidebar
```typescript
// Collapsible file explorer with icons
<Sidebar width={30} collapsible>
  <FileTree
    root={workspace}
    onSelect={handleFileSelect}
    icons={true}
    gitStatus={true}
  />
</Sidebar>
```

### Feature 4: Syntax Highlighting
```typescript
// Full syntax highlighting for code blocks
<CodeBlock
  language="typescript"
  code={content}
  theme="github-dark"
  lineNumbers={true}
  highlightLines={[5, 10, 15]}
/>
```

### Feature 5: Dashboard View
```typescript
// Stats and visualizations
<Dashboard>
  <TokenUsageChart data={tokenHistory} />
  <ModelPerformance models={availableModels} />
  <RecentSessions sessions={sessionHistory} />
</Dashboard>
```

---

## 🎭 Theme System

### Theme Structure
```typescript
interface Theme {
  name: string;
  colors: {
    background: string;
    surface: string;
    primary: string;
    secondary: string;
    accent: string;
    text: string;
    textDim: string;
    success: string;
    warning: string;
    error: string;
    border: string;
  };
  effects: {
    blur: number;
    opacity: number;
    shadow: string;
  };
  animations: {
    duration: number;
    easing: string;
  };
}
```

### Available Themes
1. **Dark** (default) - GitHub Dark aesthetic
2. **Light** - Clean, bright interface
3. **Cyberpunk** - Neon colors, high contrast
4. **Dracula** - Popular Dracula color scheme
5. **Nord** - Cool, arctic-inspired palette
6. **Solarized** - Classic Solarized Dark/Light

---

## ⌨️ Keyboard Shortcuts

### Navigation
- `Ctrl+P` - Fuzzy file search
- `Ctrl+Shift+P` - Command palette
- `Ctrl+B` - Toggle sidebar
- `Ctrl+\` - Vertical split
- `Ctrl+-` - Horizontal split
- `Ctrl+W` - Close current pane/tab
- `Ctrl+Tab` - Next tab
- `Ctrl+Shift+Tab` - Previous tab

### Editing
- `Ctrl+Z` - Undo
- `Ctrl+Y` - Redo
- `Ctrl+C` - Copy
- `Ctrl+V` - Paste
- `Ctrl+X` - Cut
- `Ctrl+A` - Select all
- `Ctrl+F` - Find in transcript

### Actions
- `Ctrl+Enter` - Submit with newline
- `Ctrl+K` - Clear transcript
- `Ctrl+L` - Clear screen
- `Ctrl+R` - Reload/refresh
- `Ctrl+S` - Save session
- `Ctrl+O` - Open session

### Vim Mode (Optional)
- `h/j/k/l` - Navigation
- `gg/G` - Top/bottom
- `/` - Search
- `n/N` - Next/previous match
- `i/a` - Insert mode
- `Esc` - Normal mode

---

## 🎬 Animation System

### Animation Types
1. **Fade In/Out** - Smooth opacity transitions
2. **Slide In/Out** - Directional slides (up/down/left/right)
3. **Scale** - Zoom in/out effects
4. **Rotate** - Spinning loaders
5. **Pulse** - Breathing animations
6. **Shimmer** - Loading skeleton screens
7. **Particles** - Background particle effects

### Usage Example
```typescript
<FadeIn duration={300} delay={100}>
  <Message content={text} />
</FadeIn>

<SlideIn direction="up" duration={400}>
  <CommandPanel />
</SlideIn>

<ParticleField
  count={50}
  color={theme.accent}
  speed={0.5}
  opacity={0.3}
/>
```

---

## 📊 Performance Optimizations

### Virtual Scrolling
- Render only visible messages (viewport)
- Handle 10,000+ messages without lag
- Smooth scrolling with momentum

### Memoization
- React.memo for all components
- useMemo for expensive calculations
- useCallback for event handlers

### Lazy Loading
- Code splitting for themes
- Dynamic imports for heavy features
- Progressive enhancement

### Debouncing
- Input debouncing (300ms)
- Resize debouncing (150ms)
- Search debouncing (200ms)

---

## 🔧 Implementation Priority

### Phase 1: Core Enhancements (Week 1)
- [x] Enhanced theme system with multiple themes
- [x] Smooth animations (fade, slide, scale)
- [x] Syntax highlighting for code blocks
- [x] Virtual scrolling for performance
- [x] Keyboard shortcuts system

### Phase 2: Advanced Features (Week 2)
- [ ] Split panes with resizing
- [ ] Tabs system for multiple sessions
- [ ] File tree sidebar
- [ ] Dashboard view with stats
- [ ] Mouse support (click, scroll, drag)

### Phase 3: Polish & UX (Week 3)
- [ ] Context menus
- [ ] Tooltips
- [ ] Fuzzy search (Ctrl+P)
- [ ] Clipboard integration
- [ ] Session persistence

### Phase 4: Advanced Polish (Week 4)
- [ ] AI autocomplete
- [ ] Rich markdown (tables, diagrams)
- [ ] Hyperlinks (clickable)
- [ ] Desktop notifications
- [ ] Screenshot export

---

## 🎯 Success Metrics

### Performance
- ✅ 60 FPS animations
- ✅ <100ms input latency
- ✅ <50MB memory usage
- ✅ Handle 10,000+ messages

### UX
- ✅ Intuitive keyboard shortcuts
- ✅ Smooth, polished animations
- ✅ Responsive to terminal resize
- ✅ Accessible (screen readers)

### Features
- ✅ 5+ themes
- ✅ Split panes
- ✅ Tabs system
- ✅ File tree
- ✅ Syntax highlighting

---

## 🚀 Getting Started

### Install Enhanced Dependencies
```bash
cd tui-ts
npm install --save \
  ink-gradient \
  ink-big-text \
  ink-select-input \
  ink-table \
  ink-progress-bar \
  prismjs \
  @types/prismjs \
  react-syntax-highlighter \
  @types/react-syntax-highlighter
```

### Run Enhanced TUI
```bash
npm run dev
```

### Try New Features
```bash
# Switch theme
/theme cyberpunk

# Open file tree
Ctrl+B

# Split pane
Ctrl+\

# New tab
Ctrl+T

# Command palette
Ctrl+Shift+P
```

---

## 📚 Resources

### Inspiration
- **GitHub CLI** - Clean, professional aesthetic
- **Vercel CLI** - Smooth animations, great UX
- **Warp Terminal** - Modern, feature-rich
- **Cursor IDE** - AI-powered features
- **VS Code** - Keyboard shortcuts, layout

### Libraries
- **Ink** - React for CLIs
- **Chalk** - Terminal colors
- **Prism.js** - Syntax highlighting
- **Ink Gradient** - Gradient text
- **Ink Big Text** - ASCII art

---

## 🎉 Conclusion

This enhancement plan will transform Elith's TUI into a **world-class terminal interface** that rivals the best modern CLIs. The focus is on:

1. **Visual Excellence** - Beautiful, polished UI
2. **Performance** - Smooth, responsive, fast
3. **Features** - Advanced, powerful, intuitive
4. **UX** - Delightful, accessible, productive

Let's build something amazing! 🚀
</content>

// Enhanced theme system with multiple beautiful themes
import gradient from 'gradient-string';

export interface Theme {
  name: string;
  colors: {
    background: string;
    surface: string;
    surfaceAlt: string;
    primary: string;
    secondary: string;
    accent: string;
    text: string;
    textDim: string;
    textDimmer: string;
    success: string;
    warning: string;
    error: string;
    info: string;
    border: string;
    borderDim: string;
    thinking: string;
    brand: string;
  };
  gradients: {
    primary: [string, string];
    accent: [string, string];
    success: [string, string];
    error: [string, string];
  };
  effects: {
    blur: number;
    opacity: number;
    glow: boolean;
  };
  animations: {
    duration: number;
    easing: string;
  };
}

// Dark Theme (Default - GitHub Dark inspired)
export const darkTheme: Theme = {
  name: 'dark',
  colors: {
    background: '#0d1117',

// Ocean Theme (Deep blue)
export const oceanTheme: Theme = {
  name: 'ocean',
  colors: {
    background: '#0c1e2e',
    surface: '#1a2f42',
    surfaceAlt: '#234056',
    primary: '#4fc3f7',
    secondary: '#29b6f6',

};

// All available themes
export const themes: Record<string, Theme> = {
  dark: darkTheme,
  light: lightTheme,
  cyberpunk: cyberpunkTheme,
  dracula: draculaTheme,
  nord: nordTheme,
  'solarized-dark': solarizedDarkTheme,
  monokai: monokaiTheme,
  ocean: oceanTheme,
};

// Default theme
export const defaultTheme = darkTheme;

// Helper to create gradient text
export function createGradient(text: string, colors: [string, string]): string {
  return gradient(colors)(text);
}

// Helper to get theme by name
export function getTheme(name: string): Theme {
  return themes[name] || defaultTheme;
}

// Helper to list all theme names
export function getThemeNames(): string[] {
  return Object.keys(themes);
}

<content>
// Theme management hook
import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import { getTheme, getThemeNames, type Theme } from '../themes/index.js';

const THEME_STORAGE_KEY = 'elith-tui-theme';

export function useTheme() {
  // Load saved theme or default to 'dark'
  const [currentThemeName, setCurrentThemeName] = useState<string>(() => {
    if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') {
      return localStorage.getItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY) || 'dark';
    }
    return 'dark';
  });

  const [theme, setTheme] = useState<Theme>(() => getTheme(currentThemeName));

  // Switch theme
  const switchTheme = useCallback((themeName: string) => {
    const newTheme = getTheme(themeName);
    setTheme(newTheme);
    setCurrentThemeName(themeName);
    
    // Save to localStorage
    if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') {
      localStorage.setItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY, themeName);
    }
  }, []);

  // Cycle to next theme
  const nextTheme = useCallback(() => {
    const themeNames = getThemeNames();
    const currentIndex = themeNames.indexOf(currentThemeName);
    const nextIndex = (currentIndex + 1) % themeNames.length;
    switchTheme(themeNames[nextIndex]);
  }, [currentThemeName, switchTheme]);

  // Cycle to previous theme
  const previousTheme = useCallback(() => {
    const themeNames = getThemeNames();
    const currentIndex = themeNames.indexOf(currentThemeName);
    const prevIndex = (currentIndex - 1 + themeNames.length) % themeNames.length;
    switchTheme(themeNames[prevIndex]);
  }, [currentThemeName, switchTheme]);

  // Get all available themes
  const availableThemes = getThemeNames();

  return {
    theme,
    currentThemeName,
    switchTheme,
    nextTheme,
    previousTheme,
    availableThemes,
  };
}
</content>

<content>
// Fade in animation component
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Box, type DOMElement } from 'ink';

interface FadeInProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  duration?: number;
  delay?: number;
}

export const FadeIn: React.FC<FadeInProps> = ({
  children,
  duration = 300,
  delay = 0,
}) => {
  const [opacity, setOpacity] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    const delayTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      const steps = 20;
      const stepDuration = duration / steps;
      let currentStep = 0;

      const interval = setInterval(() => {
        currentStep++;
        setOpacity(currentStep / steps);

        if (currentStep >= steps) {
          clearInterval(interval);
        }
      }, stepDuration);

      return () => clearInterval(interval);
    }, delay);

    return () => clearTimeout(delayTimer);
  }, [duration, delay]);

  // Note: Ink doesn't support opacity directly, so we simulate with visibility
  // For a real fade effect, we'd need to use ANSI escape codes
  if (opacity < 0.1) {
    return null;
  }

  return <Box>{children}</Box>;
};
</content>

<content>
// Enhanced spinner with multiple styles and animations
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Text } from 'ink';
import spinners from 'cli-spinners';

interface EnhancedSpinnerProps {
  type?: keyof typeof spinners;
  color?: string;
  text?: string;
}

export const EnhancedSpinner: React.FC<EnhancedSpinnerProps> = ({
  type = 'dots',
  color = 'cyan',
  text,
}) => {
  const [frame, setFrame] = useState(0);
  const spinner = spinners[type];

  useEffect(() => {
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      setFrame((prev) => (prev + 1) % spinner.frames.length);
    }, spinner.interval);

    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [spinner]);

  return (
    <>
      <Text color={color}>{spinner.frames[frame]}</Text>
      {text && <Text color="gray"> {text}</Text>}
    </>
  );
};

// Preset spinner types for different contexts
export const ThinkingSpinner: React.FC<{ text?: string }> = ({ text = 'Thinking...' }) => (
  <EnhancedSpinner type="dots12" color="magenta" text={text} />
);

export const LoadingSpinner: React.FC<{ text?: string }> = ({ text = 'Loading...' }) => (
  <EnhancedSpinner type="dots" color="cyan" text={text} />
);

export const ProcessingSpinner: React.FC<{ text?: string }> = ({ text = 'Processing...' }) => (
  <EnhancedSpinner type="arc" color="yellow" text={text} />
);

export const AnalyzingSpinner: React.FC<{ text?: string }> = ({ text = 'Analyzing...' }) => (
  <EnhancedSpinner type="bouncingBar" color="blue" text={text} />
);

export const GeneratingSpinner: React.FC<{ text?: string }> = ({ text = 'Generating...' }) => (
  <EnhancedSpinner type="growVertical" color="green" text={text} />
);
</content>

<content>
// Enhanced progress bar with animations
import React from 'react';
import { Box, Text } from 'ink';
import gradient from 'gradient-string';

interface ProgressBarProps {
  progress: number; // 0-100
  width?: number;
  color?: string;
  showPercentage?: boolean;
  label?: string;
  animated?: boolean;
}

export const ProgressBar: React.FC<ProgressBarProps> = ({
  progress,
  width = 40,
  color = 'cyan',
  showPercentage = true,
  label,
  animated = true,
}) => {
  const clampedProgress = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, progress));
  const filledWidth = Math.round((clampedProgress / 100) * width);
  const emptyWidth = width - filledWidth;

  const filled = '█'.repeat(filledWidth);
  const empty = '░'.repeat(emptyWidth);

  const bar = animated
    ? gradient(['#00ff00', '#00ffff', '#0000ff'])(filled) + empty
    : filled + empty;

  return (
    <Box flexDirection="column">
      {label && (
        <Box marginBottom={1}>
          <Text color="gray">{label}</Text>
        </Box>
      )}
      <Box>
        <Text color={color}>[{bar}]</Text>
        {showPercentage && (
          <Text color="gray"> {clampedProgress.toFixed(0)}%</Text>
        )}
      </Box>
    </Box>
  );
};

// Indeterminate progress bar (for unknown duration tasks)
export const IndeterminateProgressBar: React.FC<{
  width?: number;
  color?: string;
  label?: string;
}> = ({ width = 40, color = 'cyan', label }) => {
  const [position, setPosition] = React.useState(0);
  const barWidth = 8;

  React.useEffect(() => {
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      setPosition((prev) => (prev + 1) % (width - barWidth + 1));
    }, 50);

    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [width]);

  const before = '░'.repeat(position);
  const bar = '█'.repeat(barWidth);
  const after = '░'.repeat(width - position - barWidth);

  return (
    <Box flexDirection="column">
      {label && (
        <Box marginBottom={1}>
          <Text color="gray">{label}</Text>
        </Box>
      )}
      <Box>
        <Text color={color}>[{before}{bar}{after}]</Text>
      </Box>
    </Box>
  );
};

// Multi-step progress indicator
export const StepProgress: React.FC<{
  steps: string[];
  currentStep: number;
}> = ({ steps, currentStep }) => {
  return (
    <Box flexDirection="column">
      {steps.map((step, index) => {
        const isComplete = index < currentStep;
        const isCurrent = index === currentStep;
        const icon = isComplete ? '✓' : isCurrent ? '◉' : '○';
        const color = isComplete ? 'green' : isCurrent ? 'cyan' : 'gray';

        return (
          <Box key={index} marginBottom={index < steps.length - 1 ? 1 : 0}>
            <Text color={color}>{icon} </Text>
            <Text color={color} bold={isCurrent}>
              {step}
            </Text>
          </Box>
        );
      })}
    </Box>
  );
};
</content>

<content>
// Split pane component for side-by-side layouts
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Box, Text } from 'ink';

interface SplitPaneProps {
  children: [React.ReactNode, React.ReactNode];
  orientation?: 'horizontal' | 'vertical';
  sizes?: [number, number]; // Percentage split (e.g., [60, 40])
  minSize?: number;
  resizable?: boolean;
  borderColor?: string;
}

export const SplitPane: React.FC<SplitPaneProps> = ({
  children,
  orientation = 'vertical',
  sizes = [50, 50],
  minSize = 20,
  resizable = true,
  borderColor = 'gray',
}) => {
  const [leftSize, rightSize] = sizes;
  const [currentSizes, setCurrentSizes] = useState<[number, number]>([leftSize, rightSize]);

  // For terminal UI, we'll use fixed sizes for now
  // In a real implementation, we'd handle resize events

  if (orientation === 'vertical') {
    return (
      <Box width="100%" height="100%">
        <Box width={`${currentSizes[0]}%`} flexDirection="column" borderStyle="single" borderColor={borderColor}>
          {children[0]}
        </Box>
        <Box width={`${currentSizes[1]}%`} flexDirection="column" borderStyle="single" borderColor={borderColor}>
          {children[1]}
        </Box>
      </Box>
    );
  }

  // Horizontal orientation
  return (
    <Box flexDirection="column" width="100%" height="100%">
      <Box height={`${currentSizes[0]}%`} borderStyle="single" borderColor={borderColor}>
        {children[0]}
      </Box>
      <Box height={`${currentSizes[1]}%`} borderStyle="single" borderColor={borderColor}>
        {children[1]}
      </Box>
    </Box>
  );
};

// Triple pane layout (common for code editors)
export const TriplePaneLayout: React.FC<{
  sidebar: React.ReactNode;
  main: React.ReactNode;
  panel: React.ReactNode;
  sidebarWidth?: number;
  panelWidth?: number;
}> = ({ sidebar, main, panel, sidebarWidth = 20, panelWidth = 30 }) => {
  const mainWidth = 100 - sidebarWidth - panelWidth;

  return (
    <Box width="100%" height="100%">
      {/* Sidebar */}
      <Box width={`${sidebarWidth}%`} flexDirection="column" borderStyle="single" borderColor="gray">
        {sidebar}
      </Box>

      {/* Main content */}
      <Box width={`${mainWidth}%`} flexDirection="column" borderStyle="single" borderColor="gray">
        {main}
      </Box>

      {/* Right panel */}
      <Box width={`${panelWidth}%`} flexDirection="column" borderStyle="single" borderColor="gray">
        {panel}
      </Box>
    </Box>
  );
};

// Collapsible sidebar
export const CollapsibleSidebar: React.FC<{
  children: React.ReactNode;
  collapsed?: boolean;
  width?: number;
  onToggle?: () => void;
}> = ({ children, collapsed = false, width = 25, onToggle }) => {
  if (collapsed) {
    return (
      <Box width={3} flexDirection="column" borderStyle="single" borderColor="gray">
        <Box justifyContent="center" paddingY={1}>
          <Text color="cyan">▸</Text>
        </Box>
      </Box>
    );
  }

  return (
    <Box width={`${width}%`} flexDirection="column" borderStyle="single" borderColor="gray">
      <Box justifyContent="space-between" paddingX={1} borderStyle="single" borderColor="gray">
        <Text color="cyan" bold>Sidebar</Text>
        <Text color="gray">◂</Text>
      </Box>
      <Box flexGrow={1} flexDirection="column">
        {children}
      </Box>
    </Box>
  );
};
</content>

// Tab bar component for multiple sessions
import React from 'react';
import { Box, Text } from 'ink';

export interface Tab {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  icon?: string;
  closeable?: boolean;
  modified?: boolean;
}

interface TabBarProps {
  tabs: Tab[];
  activeTabId: string;
  onTabSelect: (tabId: string) => void;
  onTabClose?: (tabId: string) => void;
  onNewTab?: () => void;
  maxTabs?: number;
}

export const TabBar: React.FC<TabBarProps> = ({
  tabs,
  activeTabId,
  onTabSelect,
  onTabClose,
  onNewTab,
  maxTabs = 10,
}) => {
  return (
    <Box borderStyle="single" borderColor="gray" paddingX={1}>
      {/* Tabs */}
      {tabs.map((tab, index) => {
        const isActive = tab.id === activeTabId;
        const showSeparator = index < tabs.length - 1;

        return (
          <React.Fragment key={tab.id}>
            <Box
              paddingX={1}
              backgroundColor={isActive ? '#1a1a2e' : undefined}
            >
              {tab.icon && <Text color={isActive ? 'cyan' : 'gray'}>{tab.icon} </Text>}
              <Text
                color={isActive ? 'white' : 'gray'}
                bold={isActive}
              >
                {tab.title}
              </Text>
              {tab.modified && <Text color="yellow"> ●</Text>}
              {tab.closeable && (
                <Text color="gray" dimColor> ✕</Text>
              )}
            </Box>
            {showSeparator && <Text color="gray"> │ </Text>}
          </React.Fragment>
        );
      })}

      {/* New tab button */}
      {onNewTab && tabs.length < maxTabs && (
        <>
          <Text color="gray"> │ </Text>
          <Box paddingX={1}>
            <Text color="cyan">+</Text>
          </Box>
        </>
      )}

      {/* Tab count indicator */}
      <Box marginLeft={1}>
        <Text color="gray" dimColor>
          [{tabs.length}/{maxTabs}]
        </Text>
      </Box>
    </Box>
  );
};

// Tab content wrapper
export const TabContent: React.FC<{
  children: React.ReactNode;
  tabId: string;
  activeTabId: string;
}> = ({ children, tabId, activeTabId }) => {
  if (tabId !== activeTabId) {
    return null;
  }

  return <Box flexDirection="column" flexGrow={1}>{children}</Box>;
};

// Tab manager hook
export function useTabManager(initialTabs: Tab[] = []) {
  const [tabs, setTabs] = React.useState<Tab[]>(initialTabs);
  const [activeTabId, setActiveTabId] = React.useState<string>(
    initialTabs[0]?.id || ''
  );

  const addTab = React.useCallback((tab: Tab) => {
    setTabs((prev) => [...prev, tab]);
    setActiveTabId(tab.id);
  }, []);

  const removeTab = React.useCallback((tabId: string) => {
    setTabs((prev) => {
      const filtered = prev.filter((t) => t.id !== tabId);
      if (activeTabId === tabId && filtered.length > 0) {
        setActiveTabId(filtered[filtered.length - 1].id);
      }
      return filtered;
    });
  }, [activeTabId]);

  const updateTab = React.useCallback((tabId: string, updates: Partial<Tab>) => {
    setTabs((prev) =>
      prev.map((t) => (t.id === tabId ? { ...t, ...updates } : t))
    );
  }, []);

  const selectTab = React.useCallback((tabId: string) => {
    setActiveTabId(tabId);
  }, []);

  const nextTab = React.useCallback(() => {
    setTabs((prev) => {
      const currentIndex = prev.findIndex((t) => t.id === activeTabId);
      const nextIndex = (currentIndex + 1) % prev.length;
      setActiveTabId(prev[nextIndex].id);
      return prev;
    });
  }, [activeTabId]);

  const previousTab = React.useCallback(() => {
    setTabs((prev) => {
      const currentIndex = prev.findIndex((t) => t.id === activeTabId);
      const prevIndex = (currentIndex - 1 + prev.length) % prev.length;
      setActiveTabId(prev[prevIndex].id);
      return prev;
    });
  }, [activeTabId]);

  return {
    tabs,
    activeTabId,
    addTab,
    removeTab,
    updateTab,
    selectTab,
    nextTab,
    previousTab,
  };
}
</content>
